\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0,

(169)

\displaystyle\langle u|h^{TT||\alpha}+L_{Y^{||\alpha}}\gamma\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0

(170)

which upon time differentiation becomes

\displaystyle\langle\partial_{t}u|h^{TT||\alpha}+L_{Y^{||\alpha}}\gamma\rangle_{L^{2}}+second~order~terms=0,

\displaystyle\langle-\frac{2}{a}(1+\omega)k^{tr}+h^{TT||}+\frac{1}{a}X^{k}\nabla[\gamma]_{k}u+\mathcal{F}_{1}|h^{TT||\alpha}+L_{Y^{||\alpha}}\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+second~order~terms=0.

Now, in the light of elliptic estimates,
\omega
satisfies a second order estimate. Since the remaining terms are clearly of second order, using the identity
\langle A^{TT}|L_{Z}\gamma\rangle_{L^{2}}=0
for any transverse-traceless tensor
A^{TT}
and vector field
Z\in\mathfrak{X}(M)
, we immediately obtain

\displaystyle\langle\frac{2}{a}k^{tr}-h^{TT||}|h^{TT||\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}}+\langle-\frac{2}{a}\omega k^{tr}+\frac{1}{a}X^{k}\nabla[\gamma]_{k}u+\mathcal{F}_{1}|h^{TT||\alpha}+L_{Y^{||\alpha}}\rangle

\displaystyle-\langle\frac{2}{a}k^{tr}|L_{Y^{||\alpha}}\rangle=0

which leads to

\displaystyle h^{TT||}=\frac{2}{a}k^{tr||}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}(terms~linear~in~\omega)+\frac{1}{a}(second~order~terms),

(171)

where
k^{tr||}
is the projection of
k^{tr}
onto the subspace of TT tensors belonging to the kernel of
\mathcal{L}_{\gamma,\gamma}
. Now since
h^{TT||}
is an element of a finite dimensional vector space, its Sobolev norm of any order may be estimated by the
H^{s-1}
norm of
\frac{2}{a}k^{tr}
and
H^{s+1}
norm of
\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\omega
i.e.,

\displaystyle||h^{TT||}||\leq C\left(\frac{1}{a}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}\right),

(172)

where the norm on the right hand side denotes Sobolev norm of arbitrary order. Even though
\omega
is harmless, we kept the term due to the presence of
\frac{\dot{a}}{a}
factor which may be potentially dangerous. Therefore the term
\int_{\Sigma}h^{TT||}_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}
is estimated as

\displaystyle|\int_{\Sigma}h^{TT||}_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}|\leq C\left(\frac{1}{a}||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}\right).

The term
\int_{\Sigma}\mathcal{F}_{1ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

may be controlled as follows. Using the expression of
\mathcal{F}_{1ij}
(
158
), integration by parts, and
H^{s_{1}}\hookrightarrow H^{s_{2}},~s_{1}>s_{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\int_{\Sigma}\mathcal{F}_{1ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}|

(173)

\displaystyle=|\int_{\Sigma}\left(2\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\omega g_{ij}+(L_{Y^{||}+\frac{1}{a}X}\gamma)_{ij}+\frac{1}{a}u_{im}\nabla[\gamma]_{j}X^{m}+\frac{1}{a}u_{mj}\nabla[\gamma]_{i}X^{m}\right)

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}|

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||u||_{H^{s}}+||Y^{||}+\frac{1}{a}X||_{H^{s+1}}||u||_{H^{s}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}\right).

Similarly we estimate the term
\int_{\Sigma}k^{tr}_{ij}\mathcal{F}_{2kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle|\int_{\Sigma}k^{tr}_{ij}\mathcal{F}_{2kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}|

\displaystyle=|\int_{\Sigma}k^{tr}_{kl}\left(-\frac{2}{a}(1+\omega)k^{tr}_{im}k^{trm}_{j}+\left(a^{1-n\gamma_{ad}}\frac{\omega\gamma_{ad}C_{\rho}+N(\gamma_{ad}-2)\delta\rho}{n-1}+\frac{\omega}{a(n-1)}\right)g_{ij}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{2a}g^{mn}(\nabla[\gamma]_{i}u_{jm}+\nabla[\gamma]_{j}u_{im}-\nabla[\gamma]_{m}u_{ij})\nabla_{n}\omega-\frac{\dot{a}}{a}(n-2)\omega k^{tr}_{ij}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\gamma_{ad}a^{1-n\gamma_{ad}}(1+\omega)(C_{\rho}+\delta\rho)v_{i}v_{j}+\frac{1}{a}k^{tr}_{im}\nabla[\gamma]_{j}X^{m}+\frac{1}{a}k^{tr}_{mj}\nabla[\gamma]_{i}X^{m}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{a}(1+\omega)\mathfrak{J}_{ij}\right)\mu_{g}|

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\frac{1}{a}(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})||k^{tr}||^{3}_{H^{s-1}}+a^{1-n\gamma_{ad}}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+a^{1-n\gamma_{ad}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||u||_{H^{s}}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+a^{1-n\gamma_{ad}}(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})(1+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}})||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{a}(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}\right).

Collecting every individual terms,
I
computed as follows

\displaystyle I=-\frac{(n-1)\dot{a}}{a}\langle k^{tr}|k^{tr}\rangle_{L^{2}}+I_{er},

(174)

where
I_{er}
is estimated as (we keep track of the factor
1/a
)

\displaystyle|I_{er}|\leq C\left(\frac{1}{a}||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||u||_{H^{s}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||Y^{||}+\frac{1}{a}X||_{H^{s+1}}||u||_{H^{s}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}+\frac{1}{a}(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})||k^{tr}||^{3}_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+a^{1-n\gamma_{ad}}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+a^{1-n\gamma_{ad}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{a}||u||_{H^{s}}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||u||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+a^{1-n\gamma_{ad}}(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})(1+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}})||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{a}(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}\right).

Estimating terms of type II,III, and IV:
The term
II
reads

\displaystyle II=\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}[\partial_{t},\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}]u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

(175)

We will control this term as follows

\displaystyle II=\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}[\partial_{t},\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}]u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

(176)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\partial_{t}(\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl})\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}\partial_{t}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}(\partial_{t}u_{ij})\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\partial_{t}(\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g})-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}\partial_{t}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\partial_{t}(\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}\partial_{t}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g},

where we have used the self-adjoint property of
\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}
. Now we explicitly write down
\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}
and obtain

\displaystyle II=\frac{1}{8}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}(\Delta^{\gamma}_{g}u_{kl}-2R[\gamma]_{kplq}u^{pq})\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\partial_{t}(\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g})

(177)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}\partial_{t}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Sigma}g^{mn}\nabla[\gamma]_{m}u_{ij}\nabla[\gamma]_{n}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}\partial_{t}(u_{ij}R[\gamma]_{kplq}u^{pq}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\partial_{t}(\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g})-\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}\partial_{t}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}(\partial_{t}g^{mn})\nabla[\gamma]_{m}u_{ij}\nabla[\gamma]_{n}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}g^{mn}\nabla[\gamma]_{m}\partial_{t}u_{ij}\nabla[\gamma]_{n}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}g^{mn}[\partial_{t},\nabla[\gamma]_{m}]u_{ij}\nabla[\gamma]_{n}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}R[\gamma]_{kplq}\partial_{t}u^{pq}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\partial_{t}R[\gamma]_{kplq}u^{pq}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}R[\gamma]_{kplq}u^{pq}\partial_{t}(\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\partial_{t}(\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g})-\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}\partial_{t}u_{kl}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}(\partial_{t}g^{mn})\nabla[\gamma]_{m}u_{ij}\nabla[\gamma]_{n}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}g^{mn}[\partial_{t},\nabla[\gamma]_{m}]u_{ij}\nabla[\gamma]_{n}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\partial_{t}R[\gamma]_{kplq}u^{pq}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}R[\gamma]_{kplq}u^{pq}\partial_{t}(\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\mathcal{L}_{g,\gamma}u_{kl}\partial_{t}(\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g})

where we have used the fact that
\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\Delta^{\gamma}_{g}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}=\int_{\Sigma}g^{mn}\nabla[\gamma]_{m}u_{ij}\nabla[\gamma]_{n}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}
. Now we combine terms
II
,
III
, and
IV
to yield

\displaystyle II+III+IV=\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Sigma}(\partial_{t}g^{mn})\nabla[\gamma]_{m}u_{ij}\nabla[\gamma]_{n}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

(178)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}g^{mn}[\partial_{t},\nabla[\gamma]_{m}]u_{ij}\nabla[\gamma]_{n}u_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma}u_{ij}\partial_{t}R[\gamma]_{kplq}u^{pq}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma}(\frac{1}{2}k^{tr}_{ij}k^{tr}_{kl}(\partial_{t}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}+\gamma^{ik}\partial_{t}\gamma^{jl})\mu_{g}+\int_{\Sigma}\frac{1}{4}k^{tr}_{ij}k^{tr}_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\tr_{g}\partial_{t}g\mu_{g}

which using the evolution equations together with
H^{s_{1}}\hookrightarrow H^{s_{2}},~s_{1}>s_{2}
leads to the following estimate

\displaystyle|II+III+IV|\leq

(179)

\displaystyle C\left(\left[\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})+\frac{1}{a}(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||u||_{H^{s}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.(1+||u||_{H^{s}})\right](||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}+||k^{tr}||^{2}_{H^{s-1}})+(||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}+||k^{tr}||^{2}_{H^{s-1}})||\partial_{t}\gamma||_{H^{s-1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left[\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})+\frac{1}{a}(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||u||_{H^{s}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.(1+||u||_{H^{s}})\right](||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}+||k^{tr}||^{2}_{H^{s-1}})\right)

Collecting all the terms concludes the proof of the lemma.
\Box

Now we will obtain estimates for the higher order energies in a similar way as for the first order energy. Only, in this case, there will be extra innocuous commutator terms which may be estimated using the inequalities listed in section
4.1
. The following lemma provides the estimate for the total energy associated with the gravitational part.

Lemma 12:

Let
s>\frac{n}{2}+2
and
B_{\delta}(\mathbf{0})\subset H^{s}\times H^{s-1}\times H^{s-1}\times H^{s-1}
be a ball for sufficiently small
\delta>0
. Also let
(u,k^{tr},\delta\rho,v)\in B_{\delta}(\mathbf{0})
solve the re-scaled Einstein-Euler-
\Lambda
evolution equations and
E_{Ein}
be the total energy of the gravitational sector defined by equation (
85
). Then
\frac{dE_{Ein}}{dt}
satisfies the equation

\displaystyle\frac{dE_{Ein}}{dt}=-\frac{(n-1)\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{I=1}^{s}\langle k^{tr}|\mathcal{L}^{I-1}_{g,\gamma}k^{tr}\rangle_{L^{2}}+\mathcal{ER}_{s},

(180)

where
\mathcal{ER}_{s}
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{ER}_{s}\leq C\left(\frac{1}{a}||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||u||_{H^{s}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||Y^{||}+\frac{1}{a}X||_{H^{s+1}}||u||_{H^{s}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}+\frac{1}{a}(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})||k^{tr}||^{3}_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+a^{1-n\gamma_{ad}}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+a^{1-n\gamma_{ad}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{a}||u||_{H^{s}}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}||u||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+a^{1-n\gamma_{ad}}(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})(1+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}})||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||k^{tr}||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{a}(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\left[\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\frac{1}{a}(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||u||_{H^{s}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.(||u||^{2}_{H^{s}}+||k^{tr}||^{2}_{H^{s-1}})\right)

Proof:
The proof follows in an exact similar way as that of the previous lemma (lemma 11). We use the commutator estimates noted in section
4.1
to handle the terms that arise due to commuting
u
with
\mathcal{L}^{I-1}_{g,\gamma},~2\leq I\leq s
(and
k^{tr}
by
\mathcal{L}^{I-1}_{g,\gamma}
).
\Box

Now we turn our attention to estimating the fluid energy. This is the most important part and therefore we will include every detail of the calculations. Recall the following expression (
3
) from lemma 3

\displaystyle\frac{dE_{fluid}}{dt}=\frac{n\dot{a}}{a}\int_{\Sigma}N(N-1)\mathcal{C}^{0}\mu_{g}+\int_{\Sigma}\left(\partial_{t}\mathcal{C}^{0}+\frac{1}{a}\nabla_{i}\mathcal{C}^{i}+(\frac{1}{a}\nabla_{i}X^{i}+\frac{1}{N}\partial_{t}N)\mathcal{C}^{0}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{aN}\mathcal{C}^{i}\nabla_{i}N\right)N\mu_{g}

\displaystyle=\int_{\Sigma}\left(\partial_{t}\mathcal{C}^{0}+\frac{1}{a}\nabla_{i}\mathcal{C}^{i}\right)N\mu_{g}+\mathcal{ER}_{fluid1},

where
\mathcal{ER}_{fluid1}
reads

\displaystyle\mathcal{ER}_{fluid1}

(181)

\displaystyle=\frac{n\dot{a}}{a}\int_{\Sigma}N(N-1)\mathcal{C}^{0}\mu_{g}+\int_{\Sigma}\left((\frac{1}{a}\nabla_{i}X^{i}+\frac{1}{N}\partial_{t}N)\mathcal{C}^{0}+\frac{1}{aN}\mathcal{C}^{i}\nabla_{i}N\right)N\mu_{g}.

We first estimate this error term
\mathcal{ER}_{fluid1}
. The following lemma provides the necessary estimate.

Lemma 13:

Let
s>\frac{n}{2}+2
and
B_{\delta}(\mathbf{0})\subset H^{s}\times H^{s-1}\times H^{s-1}\times H^{s-1}
be a ball for sufficiently small
\delta>0
. Also let
(u,k^{tr},\delta\rho,v)\in B_{\delta}(\mathbf{0})
solve the re-scaled Einstein-Euler-
\Lambda
evolution equations. Then the following estimate holds for the fluid energy error term
\mathcal{ER}_{fluid1}

\displaystyle|\mathcal{ER}_{fluid1}|\leq C\left([\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})\right.

(182)

\displaystyle\left.+(1+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}})||\partial_{t}N||_{H^{s}}]E_{fluid}+\frac{1}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}\left\{(1+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}})||v||_{H^{s-1}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.||\delta\rho||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+||v||_{H^{s-1}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+||v||^{3}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})(1+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}})\right\}\right)

Proof:
Noting
N-1=\omega
and
-\int_{\Sigma}\mathcal{C}^{\mu}n_{\mu}\mu_{g}=\int_{\Sigma}N\mathcal{C}^{0}\mu_{g}=E_{fluid}
, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{ER}_{fluid1}|\leq\left(\frac{n\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{L^{\infty}}+\frac{1}{a}||\nabla_{i}X^{i}||_{L^{\infty}}+||\frac{1}{N}\partial_{t}N||_{L^{\infty}}\right)E_{fluid}

(183)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{a}||\nabla\omega||_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\Sigma}|\mathcal{C}|\mu_{g}.

Now writing
\nabla_{i}X^{i}=\nabla[\gamma]_{i}X^{i}+(\Gamma[g]^{k}_{kl}-\Gamma[\gamma]^{k}_{kl})X^{l}
, using the expression for
\mathcal{C}^{i}
(
83
)

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{i}:=\frac{(\gamma_{ad}-1)v^{i}\dot{\rho}^{2}}{\gamma_{ad}\rho}+2(\gamma_{ad}-1)\dot{v}^{i}\dot{\rho}+\gamma_{ad}\rho v^{i}(-\frac{g(v,\dot{v})^{2}}{1+g(v,v)}+g_{ij}\dot{v}^{i}\dot{v}^{j}),

(184)

and using Sobolev embedding, we obtain the desired estimate.
\Box

Now we will estimate
\int_{\Sigma}\left(\partial_{t}\mathcal{C}^{0}+\frac{1}{a}\nabla_{i}\mathcal{C}^{i}\right)N\mu_{g}
, the most important term in the fluid energy. The following lemma provides the necessary estimates for this term. The proof of this lemma is the longest one of this article. We will carefully keep track of each and every term and show the explicit calculations behind the estimates obtained. The most important point to note here is that there are terms that are multiplied by
\frac{\dot{a}}{a}
which may be potentially dangerous. We will keep track of these terms very carefully.

Lemma 14:

Let
s>\frac{n}{2}+2
and
B_{\delta}(\mathbf{0})\subset H^{s}\times H^{s-1}\times H^{s-1}\times H^{s-1}
be a ball for sufficiently small
\delta>0
. Also let
(u,k^{tr},\delta\rho,v)\in B_{\delta}(\mathbf{0})
solve the re-scaled Einstein-Euler-
\Lambda
evolution equations. The principal terms
\int_{\Sigma}\left(\partial_{t}\mathcal{C}^{0}+\frac{1}{a}\nabla_{i}\mathcal{C}^{i}\right)N\mu_{g}
verifies the following equation

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma}\left(\partial_{t}\mathcal{C}^{0}+\frac{1}{a}\nabla_{i}\mathcal{C}^{i}\right)N\mu_{g}=-2\int_{\Sigma}\gamma_{ad}\rho Nu^{0}[2N-1-n(\gamma_{ad}-1)]\frac{\dot{a}}{a}g(\dot{v},\dot{v})\mu_{g}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{ER}_{fluid2},

where
\mathcal{ER}_{fluid2}
satisfies the estimate

\displaystyle|\mathcal{ER}_{fluid2}|\leq C\left(\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}\left[1+||u||_{H^{s}}+||v||_{H^{s-1}}+||v||^{2}_{H{s-1}}+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}||u||_{H^{s}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+||u||_{H^{s}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}\right]||\delta\rho||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+||v||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left[||\partial_{t}\gamma||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}||\partial_{t}v||_{H^{s-2}}+\frac{1}{a}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}+\frac{1}{a}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\frac{1}{a}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}||u||_{H^{s}}+||\partial_{t}\gamma||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+||\partial_{t}\gamma||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||v||_{H^{s-1}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}||X||_{H^{s+1}}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||v||^{4}_{H^{s-1}}+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}\left[||\partial_{t}\gamma||_{H^{s-1}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{||v||_{H^{s-1}}}{a}(||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}})+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}||\partial_{t}\gamma||_{H^{s-1}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+||\partial_{t}v||_{H^{s-2}}(||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}+||u||_{H^{s}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}+||v||_{H^{s-1}})\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\frac{1}{a}||v||_{H^{s-1}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||X||_{H^{s+1}}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{a}||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\frac{1}{a}||v||_{H^{s-1}}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}||\omega||_{H^{s+1}}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.+[||\partial_{t}g||_{H^{s-1}}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+||v||_{H^{s-1}}||\partial_{t}v||_{H^{s-2}}+||\partial_{t}N||_{H^{s}}+||\partial_{t}\delta\rho||_{H^{s-2}}]||\delta\rho||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+[||\partial_{t}N||_{H^{s}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}||\partial_{t}g||_{H^{s-1}}+||v||_{H^{s-1}}||\partial_{t}v||_{H^{s-2}}]||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}[||\partial_{t}\delta\rho||_{H^{s-2}}+||\partial_{t}g||_{H^{s-1}}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+||v||_{H^{s-1}}||\partial_{t}v||_{H^{s-2}}+||\partial_{t}N||_{H^{s}}]\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||\partial_{t}g||_{H^{s-1}}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}[||\partial_{t}g||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}+||v||_{H^{s-1}}||\partial_{t}v||_{H^{s-2}}]\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{a}\left[||v||_{H^{s-1}}||\delta\rho||^{3}_{H^{s-1}}+||v||_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})||\delta\rho||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+||v||^{3}_{H^{s-1}}(||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+||u||_{H^{s}})\right]\right)

Proof:
Recall from section 3, the expression (
113
) for
\int_{\Sigma}\left(\partial_{t}\mathcal{C}^{0}+\frac{1}{a}\nabla_{i}\mathcal{C}^{i}\right)N\mu_{g}

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma}(\partial_{t}\mathcal{C}^{0}+\frac{1}{a}\nabla_{i}\mathcal{C}^{i})N\mu_{g}

(185)

\displaystyle=\int_{\Sigma}\left(\underbrace{\frac{2(\gamma_{ad}-1)(1+g(v,v))^{1/2}}{a\gamma_{ad}\rho N}\dot{\rho}L_{X}\dot{\rho}}_{I}-\underbrace{\frac{2\gamma_{ad}\rho g(v,\dot{v})}{aN[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}g(v,L_{X}\dot{v})}_{II}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\underbrace{\frac{2g(v,\dot{v})}{N[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}g(v,\frac{\mathcal{T}_{2}}{u^{0}})}_{IM_{2}}+\underbrace{\frac{2\gamma_{ad}\rho[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{aN}g(\dot{v},L_{X}\dot{v})}_{III}+\underbrace{\frac{2[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{N}g(\dot{v},\frac{\mathcal{T}_{2}}{u^{0}})}_{IM_{1}:=important~term}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{2(\gamma_{ad}-1)}{aN[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}\left(\underbrace{L_{X}(\dot{\rho}g(v,\dot{v}))}_{IV}+\dot{\rho}g(X^{k}\nabla_{k}v,\dot{v})+\dot{\rho}g(v,\dot{v}^{k}\nabla_{k}X)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\underbrace{\frac{2(\gamma_{ad}-1)[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{\gamma_{ad}\rho N}\dot{\rho}\mathcal{T}_{1}}_{IM_{3}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\gamma_{ad}-1}{\gamma_{ad}}\left(\frac{[1+g(v,v)]^{-1/2}}{2\rho N}(\partial_{t}g(v,v)+2g(\partial_{t}v,v))-\frac{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{\rho^{2}N^{2}}[\rho\partial_{t}N+N\partial_{t}\rho]\right)\dot{\rho}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2(\gamma_{ad}-1)\dot{\rho}\dot{v}^{j}\left(-\frac{\partial_{t}Ng_{ij}v^{i}}{N^{2}[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}-\frac{g_{ij}v^{i}}{N[1+g(v,v)]^{3/2}}[\partial_{t}g(v,v)+2g(\partial_{t}v,v)]\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\frac{1}{N[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}(\partial_{t}g_{ij}v^{i}+g_{ij}\partial_{t}v^{i})\right)+\gamma_{ad}\left[g(\dot{v},\dot{v})-\frac{g(v,\dot{v})^{2}}{1+g(v,v)}\right]\left(\frac{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{N}\partial_{t}\rho\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\frac{\rho[1+g(v,v)]^{-1/2}}{2N}(\partial_{t}g(v,v)+2g(\partial_{t}v,v))-\frac{\rho[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{N^{2}}\partial_{t}N\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\gamma_{ad}\rho[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{N}\left[(\partial_{t}g_{ij})\dot{v}^{i}\dot{v}^{j}+\frac{g(v,\dot{v})^{2}}{[1+g(v,v)]^{2}}(\partial_{t}g(v,v)+2g(\partial_{t}v,v))\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\frac{g(v,\dot{v})}{1+g(v,v)}(\partial_{t}g_{ij}v^{i}\dot{v}^{j}+g_{ij}\dot{v}^{i}\partial_{t}v^{j})\right]+\frac{1}{a}\left(-\frac{\gamma_{ad}-1}{\gamma_{ad}}\frac{\dot{\rho}^{2}}{\rho^{2}}v^{i}\nabla_{i}\rho+\frac{\gamma_{ad}-1}{\gamma_{ad}}\frac{\dot{\rho}^{2}}{\rho}\nabla_{i}v^{i}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\gamma_{ad}[v^{i}\nabla_{i}\rho+\rho\nabla_{i}v^{i}]\left[-\frac{g(v,\dot{v})^{2}}{1+g(v,v)}+g(\dot{v},\dot{v})\right]+\gamma_{ad}\rho v^{i}\left(\frac{2g(v,\nabla_{i}v)g(v,\dot{v})^{2}}{[1+g(v,v)]^{2}}\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\left.-\frac{2g(v,\dot{v})g(\dot{v},\nabla_{i}v)}{1+g(v,v)}\right)\right)\right)N\mu_{g}.

As we mentioned previously, the terms
I
,
II
,
III
, and
IV
contain derivatives of
\dot{\rho}
and
\dot{v}
in the direction of the shift vector field
X
. We will reduce these derivative terms to lower order terms by integration by parts. We proceed sequentially starting with the term
I

\displaystyle I=\frac{2(\gamma_{ad}-1)}{a\gamma_{ad}}\int\frac{(1+g(v,v))^{1/2}}{\rho N}\dot{\rho}L_{X}\dot{\rho}N\mu_{g}

(186)

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma_{ad}-1}{a\gamma_{ad}}\int\left(L_{X}(\frac{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}\dot{\rho}^{2}}{\rho})-\dot{\rho}^{2}L_{X}(\frac{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{\rho})\right)\mu_{g}

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma_{ad}-1}{a\gamma_{ad}}\int\left(\nabla_{i}(\frac{\dot{\rho}^{2}[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{\rho}X^{i})-\frac{\dot{\rho}^{2}[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{\rho}\nabla_{i}X^{i}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\dot{\rho}^{2}[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{\rho^{2}}L_{X}\rho-\frac{\dot{\rho}^{2}}{\rho}L_{X}[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}\right)\mu_{g}

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma_{ad}-1}{a\gamma_{ad}}\int\left(-\frac{\dot{\rho}^{2}[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{\rho}\nabla_{i}X^{i}+\frac{\dot{\rho}^{2}[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{\rho^{2}}L_{X}\rho\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{\dot{\rho}^{2}}{\rho}L_{X}[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}\right)\mu_{g},

where we have used Stokes’s theorem on the closed manifold
\Sigma
. Notice that the directional derivative of
\dot{\rho}
has disappeared. Using the usual trick, we now express
\nabla_{i}X^{i}
as follows

\displaystyle\nabla_{i}X^{i}=\nabla[\gamma]_{i}X^{i}+(\Gamma[g]^{i}_{ik}-\Gamma[\gamma]^{i}_{ik})X^{k}

(187)

\displaystyle=\nabla[\gamma]_{i}X^{i}+\frac{1}{2}g^{il}\left(\nabla[\gamma]_{i}g_{lk}+\nabla[\gamma]_{k}g_{il}-\nabla[\gamma]_{l}g_{ik}\right)X^{k}

\displaystyle=\nabla[\gamma]_{i}X^{i}+\frac{1}{2}g^{il}\nabla[\gamma]_{k}g_{il}.

Substituting the expression (
187
) of
\nabla_{i}X^{i}
together with elementary calculations yield the following

\displaystyle I=\frac{\gamma_{ad}-1}{a\gamma_{ad}}\int\left(-\frac{\dot{\rho}^{2}[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{\rho}(\nabla[\gamma]_{i}X^{i}+\frac{1}{2}g^{il}\nabla[\gamma]_{k}g_{il})\right.

(188)

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\dot{\rho}^{2}[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{\rho^{2}}L_{X}\rho-\frac{\dot{\rho}^{2}}{\rho}[1+g(v,v)]^{-1/2}g_{ij}X^{k}v^{j}\left(\nabla[\gamma]_{k}v^{i}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\frac{1}{2}g^{il}(\nabla[\gamma]_{k}g_{lm}+\nabla[\gamma]_{m}g_{kl}-\nabla[\gamma]_{l}g_{km})v^{m}\right)\right)\mu_{g}.

Utilizing inequalities (
114
-
118
) and Sobolev inequalities for
s>\frac{n}{2}+2
, we estimate
I
as follows

\displaystyle|I|\leq C\frac{\gamma_{ad}-1}{a\gamma_{ad}}\left(||\frac{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{C_{\rho}+\delta\rho}||_{L^{\infty}}(||\nabla[\gamma]X||_{L^{\infty}}+||X||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}})\right.

(189)

\displaystyle\left.+||\frac{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{(C_{\rho}+\delta\rho)^{2}}||_{L^{\infty}}||X||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla\rho||_{L^{\infty}}+||\frac{[1+g(v,v)]^{-1/2}}{C_{\rho}+\delta\rho}||_{L^{\infty}}||X||_{L^{\infty}}||v||_{L^{\infty}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.(1+||u||_{L^{\infty}})(||\nabla[\gamma]v||_{L^{\infty}}+||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}}||v||_{L^{\infty}}\right)E_{fluid}

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\gamma_{ad}-1}{a\gamma_{ad}}\left((1+||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+||u||_{L^{\infty}}||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}})(C_{\rho}+||\delta\rho||_{L^{\infty}})(||\nabla[\gamma]X||_{L^{\infty}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||X||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}})+(1+||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+||u||_{L^{\infty}}||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}})(C^{2}_{\rho}+||\delta\rho||^{2}_{L^{\infty}})||X||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla\delta\rho||_{L^{\infty}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||X||_{L^{\infty}}||v||_{L^{\infty}}(1+||u||_{L^{\infty}})(||\nabla[\gamma]v||_{L^{\infty}}+||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}}||v||_{L^{\infty}})\right)E_{fluid}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\gamma_{ad}-1)}{a\gamma_{ad}}(1+||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+||u||_{L^{\infty}}||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}})\left((1+||\delta\rho||_{L^{\infty}})(||\nabla[\gamma]X||_{L^{\infty}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||X||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}})+(1+||\delta\rho||^{2}_{L^{\infty}})||X||_{L^{\infty}}||v||_{L^{\infty}}(1+||u||_{L^{\infty}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.(||\nabla[\gamma]v||_{L^{\infty}}+||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}}||v||_{L^{\infty}})\right)E_{fluid}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\gamma_{ad}-1)}{a\gamma_{ad}}(1+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+||u||_{H^{s}}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}})\left((1+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.(||X||_{H^{s+1}}+||X||_{H^{s+1}}||u||_{H^{s-1}})+(1+||\delta\rho||^{2}_{H^{s-1}})||X||_{H^{s+1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.(1+||u||_{H^{s}})(||v||_{H^{s-1}}+||u||_{H^{s}}||v||_{H^{s-1}})\right)E_{fluid}.

Now we need to estimate term
II
. In order to do so, first note the following calculations

\displaystyle g(v,\dot{v})g(v,L_{X}\dot{v})=g(v,\dot{v})g(v,X^{k}\nabla_{k}\dot{v}-\dot{v}^{k}\nabla_{k}X)

(190)

\displaystyle=g(v,\dot{v})X^{k}\nabla_{k}g(v,\dot{v})-g(v,\dot{v})g(v,\dot{v}^{k}\nabla_{k}X)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}X^{k}\nabla_{k}\left((g(v,\dot{v}))^{2}\right)-g(v,\dot{v})g(v,\dot{v}^{k}\nabla_{k}X),

substitution of which together with the usual integration by parts argument yield

\displaystyle II=\frac{2\gamma_{ad}}{a}\int\frac{\rho g(v,\dot{v})}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}g(v,L_{X}\dot{v})\mu_{g}

(191)

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma_{ad}}{a}\int\frac{X^{k}\nabla_{k}(g(v,\dot{v})^{2})}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}\mu_{g}-\frac{2\gamma_{ad}}{a}\int\frac{g(v,\dot{v})g(v,\dot{v}^{k}\nabla_{k}X)}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\gamma_{ad}}{a}\int g(v,\dot{v})^{2}\nabla_{k}\left(\frac{X^{k}}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}\right)\mu_{g}-\frac{2\gamma_{ad}}{a}\int\frac{g(v,\dot{v})g(v,\dot{v}^{k}\nabla_{k}X)}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\gamma_{ad}}{a}\int\left(\frac{g(v,\dot{v})^{2}}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}\nabla_{k}X^{k}-\frac{g(v,\dot{v})^{2}X^{k}g(\nabla_{k}v,v)}{[1+g(v,v)]^{3/2}}\right)\mu_{g}

\displaystyle-\frac{2\gamma_{ad}}{a}\int\frac{g(v,\dot{v})g(v,\dot{v}^{k}\nabla_{k}X)}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}\mu_{g}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\gamma_{ad}}{a}\int\left(\frac{g(v,\dot{v})^{2}}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}(\nabla[\gamma]_{k}X^{k}+(\Gamma[g]^{k}_{kj}-\Gamma[\gamma]^{k}_{kj})X^{k})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{g(v,\dot{v})^{2}X^{k}g(\nabla_{k}v,v)}{[1+g(v,v)]^{3/2}}\right)\mu_{g}-\frac{2\gamma_{ad}}{a}\int\frac{g(v,\dot{v})g(v,\dot{v}^{k}\nabla_{k}X)}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}\mu_{g}.

Therefore
II
is estimated as follows

\displaystyle|II|\leq\frac{C\gamma_{ad}}{a}\left\{||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}(||\nabla[\gamma]X||_{L^{\infty}}+||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}}||X||_{L^{\infty}})+||X||_{L^{\infty}}||v||_{L^{\infty}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.(||\nabla[\gamma]v||_{L^{\infty}}+||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}}||v||_{L^{\infty}})+||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}(||\nabla[\gamma]X||_{L^{\infty}}+||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}}||X||_{L^{\infty}})\right\}E_{fluid}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\gamma_{ad}}{a}\left\{||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(||X||_{H^{s+1}}+||u||_{H^{s}}||X||_{X^{s+1}})+||X||_{H^{s+1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.(||v||_{H^{s-1}}+||u||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}})+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(||X||_{H^{s+1}}+||u||_{H^{s}}||X||_{H^{s+1}})\right\}E_{fluid}.

Expressing the covariant derivatives with respect to the metric
g
in terms of those of the metric
\gamma
i.e.,
\nabla_{k}v^{i}=\nabla[\gamma]_{k}v^{i}+(\Gamma[g]^{i}_{kj}-\Gamma[\gamma]^{i}_{kj})v^{j}
, the term
III
reads

\displaystyle III=-\frac{\gamma_{ad}}{a}\int_{\Sigma}g(\dot{v},\dot{v})\left(\rho\frac{X^{k}g_{ij}v^{i}(\nabla[\gamma]_{k}v^{j}+(\Gamma[g]^{j}_{kl}-\Gamma[\gamma]^{j}_{jl})v^{l})}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}+\rho[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.(\nabla[\gamma]_{k}X^{k}+(\Gamma[g]^{k}_{kl}-\Gamma[\gamma]^{k}_{kl})X^{l})+[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}X^{k}\nabla_{k}\rho\right)\mu_{g}

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma_{ad}}{a}\int_{\Sigma}\rho[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}g_{ij}\dot{v}^{i}\dot{v}^{k}(\nabla[\gamma]_{k}X^{j}+(\Gamma[g]^{j}_{kl}-\Gamma[\gamma]^{j}_{kl})X^{l})\mu_{g},

which is estimated as follows

\displaystyle|III|\leq\frac{C}{a}\left(||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}}||X||_{L^{\infty}}+||X||_{L^{\infty}}||v||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla[\gamma]v||_{L^{\infty}}\right.

(192)

\displaystyle\left.+(1+||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}||u||_{L^{\infty}})(||\rho||_{L^{\infty}}(||\nabla[\gamma]X||_{L^{\infty}}+||X||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||X||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla w||_{L^{\infty}})\right)E_{fluid}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left(||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}||u||_{H^{s}}||X||_{H^{s+1}}+||X||_{H^{s+1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+(1+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}||u||_{H^{s}})((1+||\delta\rho||_{L^{H^{s-1}}})(||X||_{H^{s+1}}+||X||_{H^{s+1}}||u||_{H^{s}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||X||_{H^{s+1}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}})\right)E_{fluid}.

Similarly, the term
IV

\displaystyle IV=-\int_{\Sigma}\frac{2(\gamma_{ad}-1)}{a[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}L_{X}(\dot{\rho}g(v,\dot{v}))\mu_{g}

(193)

\displaystyle=\frac{2(\gamma_{ad}-1)}{a}\int_{\Sigma}\dot{\rho}g(v,\dot{v})\nabla_{k}(\frac{X^{k}}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}})\mu_{g}

\displaystyle=\frac{2(\gamma_{ad}-1)}{a}\int_{\Sigma}\dot{\rho}g(v,\dot{v})\left(\frac{\nabla[\gamma]_{k}X^{k}+(\Gamma[g]^{k}_{ki}-\Gamma[\gamma]^{k}_{ki})X^{i})}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}-\frac{X^{k}g(v,\nabla_{k}v)}{[1+g(v,v)]^{3/2}}\right)\mu_{g}

is estimated as

\displaystyle|IV|\leq\frac{C(\gamma_{ad}-1)}{a}||\left(\frac{\nabla[\gamma]_{k}X^{k}+(\Gamma[g]^{k}_{ki}-\Gamma[\gamma]^{k}_{ki})X^{i})}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}-\frac{X^{k}g(v,\nabla_{k}v)}{[1+g(v,v)]^{3/2}}\right)||_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma}(\dot{\rho}^{2}+g(v,\dot{v})^{2})\mu_{g}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\gamma_{ad}-1)}{a}\left(||X||_{H^{s+1}}+||X||_{H^{s+1}}||u||_{H^{s}}+||X||_{H^{s+1}}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})\right)

\displaystyle(1+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}}))E_{fluid}.

Now we will control the remaining terms. One of the terms that is most important to us is the following

\displaystyle IM_{1}:=\int_{\Sigma}\frac{2[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{N}g(\dot{v},\frac{\mathcal{T}_{2}}{u^{0}})N\mu_{g},

(194)

which upon using the normalization condition
Nu^{0}=[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}
yields

\displaystyle IM_{1}=\int_{\Sigma}\frac{2[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{N}g(\dot{v},\frac{\mathcal{T}_{2}}{u^{0}})N\mu_{g}=2\int_{\Sigma}Ng(\dot{v},\mathcal{T}_{2})\mu_{g}.

(195)

Now recall the expression for
\mathcal{T}_{2}
from equation (
82
)

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{i}_{2}:=-\underbrace{\gamma_{ad}\rho u^{0}[2N-1-n(\gamma_{ad}-1)]\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\dot{v}^{i}}_{one~of~the~most~important~term:~generates~decay}

(196)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\gamma_{ad}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\rho u^{0}\partial_{t}]v^{i}}_{i_{1}}-\underbrace{\gamma_{ad}\left[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\rho u^{0}[2N-1-n(\gamma_{ad}-1)]\right]v^{i}}_{i_{2}}+\underbrace{\frac{2\gamma_{ad}}{a}\mathcal{D}^{s-1}[\rho Nu^{0}k^{tri}_{j}v^{j}]}_{i_{3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{\gamma_{ad}}{a}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\rho u^{0}L_{X}]v^{i}}_{i_{4}}-\underbrace{\frac{\gamma_{ad}}{a}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\rho v^{j}\nabla_{j}]v^{i}}_{i_{5}}+\underbrace{\frac{\gamma_{ad}}{a}\mathcal{D}^{s-1}\left[\rho[1+g(v,v)]\nabla^{i}N\right]}_{i_{6}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(\gamma_{ad}-1)}{a}\left\{\underbrace{[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\nabla^{i}]\rho}_{i_{7}}+\underbrace{[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},v^{i}L_{v}]\rho}_{i_{8}}+\underbrace{a[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},u^{0}v^{i}\partial_{t}]\rho}_{i_{9}}-\underbrace{[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},u^{0}v^{i}L_{X}]\rho}_{i_{10}}\right\}

\displaystyle=-\underbrace{\gamma_{ad}\rho u^{0}[2N-1-n(\gamma_{ad}-1)]\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\dot{v}^{i}}_{one~of~the~most~important~term:~generates~decay}+\mathcal{T}^{i}_{2remainder}

(197)

and substitute in the expression of
IM_{1}
to yield

\displaystyle IM_{1}=-2\int_{\Sigma}\gamma_{ad}\rho Nu^{0}[2N-1-n(\gamma_{ad}-1)]\frac{\dot{a}}{a}g(\dot{v},\dot{v})\mu_{g}

(198)

\displaystyle+2\int_{\Sigma}Ng(\dot{v},\mathcal{T}_{2remainder})\mu_{g}.

This essentially yields the first part of the lemma. Now We will estimate the term
2\int_{\Sigma}Ng(\dot{v},\mathcal{T}_{2remainder})\mu_{g}
as follows

\displaystyle|\int_{\Sigma}Ng(\dot{v},\mathcal{T}_{2remainder})\mu_{g}|

(199)

\displaystyle\leq(1+||u||_{L^{\infty}})||\dot{v}||_{L^{2}}||N\mathcal{T}_{2remainder}||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+||u||_{H^{s}})||\dot{v}||_{L^{2}}||N\mathcal{T}_{2remainder}||_{L^{2}}.

In order to obtain
||\mathcal{T}_{2remainder}||_{L^{2}}
, we use its expression (
197
) and estimate each term involved i.e., terms
i_{1}
throuh
i_{10}
. In doing so we use the commutator estimates (
115
), the composition estimate (
117
), and the Sobolev inequalities for
s>\frac{n}{2}+2
.

\displaystyle||i_{1}||_{L^{2}}=||-N\gamma_{ad}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\rho u^{0}\partial_{t}]v^{i}||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq||N\rho u^{0}\sum_{l=1}^{s-1}\mathcal{D}^{s-1-l}((T_{\gamma}\mathcal{D}\cdot\partial_{t}\gamma)\mathcal{D}^{l-1}v^{i})||_{L^{2}}+||[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},(\rho u^{0}-C_{\rho})](\partial_{t}v^{i})||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left((C_{\rho}+||\delta\rho||_{L^{\infty}})(||Nu^{0}-1||_{L^{\infty}}+1)||\partial_{t}\gamma||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(||\nabla[\gamma](N\rho u^{0}-C_{\rho})||_{L^{\infty}}||\partial_{t}v^{i}||_{H^{s-1}}+||N\rho u^{0}-C_{\rho}||_{H^{s-1}}||\partial_{t}v^{i}||_{L^{\infty}}\right)\right)

Now utilizing the Sobolev embedding and composition estimate (
117
), we obtain for small data

\displaystyle||Nu^{0}-1||_{L^{\infty}}\leq C||Nu^{0}-1||_{H^{s-1}}\leq C||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}}),

(200)

which yields

\displaystyle||i_{1}||_{L^{2}}\leq C\left((C_{\rho}+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}})(||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})+1)||\partial_{t}\gamma||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}(1+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}}))||\partial_{t}v||_{H^{s-2}}\right)\right).

Similarly, we estimate

\displaystyle||i_{2}||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=||\left([\mathcal{D}^{s-1},(\rho u^{0}-C_{\rho})\left(2(N-1)-n(\gamma_{ad}-\frac{n+1}{n})]\right)v^{i}+[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},2C_{\rho}(N-1)]v^{i}\right)||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(||\rho u^{0}-C_{\rho}||_{H^{s-1}}(1+||N-1||_{H^{s-1}})+||N-1||_{H^{s-1}}\right)||v||_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||N-1||_{H^{s-1}})+||N-1||_{H^{s-1}}\right)||v||_{H^{s-1}},

\displaystyle||i_{3}||_{L^{2}}=\frac{1}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{s-1}(\rho(Nu^{0}-1)k^{tri}_{j}v^{j}+\rho k^{tri}_{j}v^{j})||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left(||\rho-C_{\rho}||_{H^{s-1}}||Nu^{0}-1||_{H^{s-1}}+||\rho-C_{\rho}||_{H^{s-1}}+||Nu^{0}-1||_{H^{s-1}}+1\right)

\displaystyle(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}})||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left(||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}})+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}})+1\right)

\displaystyle(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}})||k^{tr}||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}},

\displaystyle||Ni_{4}||_{L^{2}}=\frac{1}{a}||N[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\rho u^{0}L_{X}]v^{i}||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left(||N\rho u^{0}||_{L^{\infty}}||X^{k}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\nabla[\gamma]_{k}]v^{i}+[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},X^{k}]\nabla[\gamma]_{k}v^{i}-v^{k}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\nabla[\gamma]_{k}]X^{i}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},v^{k}]\nabla[\gamma]_{k}X^{i}||_{L^{2}}+||([\mathcal{D}^{s-1},(N\rho u^{0}-C_{\rho})](X^{k}\nabla[\gamma]_{k}v^{i}-v^{k}\nabla[\gamma]_{k}X^{i}))||_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left((1+||\delta\rho||_{L^{\infty}}(1+||Nu^{0}-1||_{L^{\infty}})+||Nu^{0}-1||_{L^{\infty}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.(||X||_{L^{\infty}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}+||\nabla[\gamma]X||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla[\gamma]v||_{H^{s-2}}+||v||_{L^{\infty}}||X||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||X||_{H^{s-1}}||\nabla[\gamma]v||_{L^{\infty}}+||v||_{H^{s-1}}||\nabla[\gamma]X||_{L^{\infty}})+\{||\nabla(N\rho u^{0}-C_{\rho})||_{L^{\infty}}||X^{k}\nabla[\gamma]_{k}v^{i}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-v^{k}\nabla[\gamma]_{k}X^{i}||_{H^{s-1}}+||N\rho u^{0}-C_{\rho}||_{H^{s-1}}||X^{k}\nabla[\gamma]_{k}v^{i}-v^{k}\nabla[\gamma]_{k}X^{i}||_{L^{\infty}}\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left((1+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}(1+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}}))+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}}))\right.

\displaystyle\left.||X||_{H^{s+1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}(1+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}})+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}}))\right.

\displaystyle\left.||X||_{H^{s+1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}\right)

\displaystyle||i_{5}||_{L^{2}}=\frac{1}{a}||[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\rho v^{k}\nabla_{k}]v^{i}||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}||\left(\rho v^{k}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\nabla[\gamma]_{k}]v^{i}+\rho v^{k}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},(\Gamma[g]-\Gamma[\gamma])_{k}]v^{i}+[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\rho v^{k}]\right.

\displaystyle\left.(\nabla[\gamma]_{k}v^{i}+(\Gamma[g]^{i}_{kl}-\Gamma[\gamma]^{i}_{kl})v^{l})\right)||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left(||\rho v||_{L^{\infty}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}+||\nabla[\gamma]\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{H^{s-1}}||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||\nabla[\gamma](\rho v)||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla[\gamma]v||_{H^{s-2}}+||\rho v||_{H^{s-1}}||\nabla v||_{L^{\infty}}+||\nabla[\gamma](\rho v)||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{H^{s-2}}||v||_{L^{\infty}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||\rho v||_{H^{s-1}}||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}}||v||_{L^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left((1+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}})||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}||u|||_{H^{s}}+(1+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}})||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}||u||_{H^{s-1}}\right),

\displaystyle||i_{6}||_{L^{2}}=\frac{1}{a}||\mathcal{D}^{s-1}\left(\rho(1+g(v,v))g^{ij}\nabla_{j}N\right)||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left((1+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}})(1+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}}))||N-1||_{H^{s}}\right),

\displaystyle||i_{7}||_{L^{2}}=\frac{1}{a}||[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},g^{ij}\nabla[\gamma]_{j}]\rho||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{a}||[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},g^{ij}\nabla[\gamma]_{j}]\delta\rho||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left(||\nabla\delta\rho||_{L^{\infty}}+||\nabla[\gamma]u||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+||u||_{H^{s-1}}||\nabla\delta\rho||_{L^{\infty}}+||u||_{L^{\infty}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left(||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+||u||_{H^{s}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}\right),

\displaystyle||i_{8}||_{L^{2}}=\frac{1}{a}||[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},v^{i}L_{v}]\rho||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{a}||[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},v^{i}L_{v}]\delta\rho||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left(||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+||v\nabla[\gamma]v||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla\delta\rho||_{H^{s-2}}+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}||\nabla\delta\rho||_{L^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}},

\displaystyle||Ni_{9}||_{L^{2}}=||N[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},u^{0}v^{i}\partial_{t}]\delta\rho||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(||Nu^{0}v^{i}\sum_{l=1}^{s-1}\mathcal{D}^{s-1-l}((T_{\gamma}\mathcal{D}\cdot\partial_{t}\gamma)\mathcal{D}^{l-1}\delta\rho)||_{L^{2}}+||N[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},u^{0}v^{i}]\partial_{t}\rho||_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(||v||_{L^{\infty}}(1+||Nu^{0}-1||_{L^{\infty}})||\partial_{t}\gamma||_{H^{s-1}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+(1+||N-1||_{L^{\infty}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.(||\nabla[\gamma](Nu^{0}v^{i})||_{L^{\infty}}||\partial_{t}\rho||_{H^{s-2}}+||Nu^{0}v^{i}||_{H^{s-1}}||\partial_{t}\rho||_{L^{\infty}})\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(||v||_{H^{s-1}}(1+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}})))||\partial_{t}\gamma||_{H^{s-1}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+(1+||N-1||_{H^{s+1}})||v||_{H^{s-1}}(1+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}}))||\partial_{t}\rho||_{H^{s-2}}\right),

\displaystyle||Ni_{10}||_{L^{2}}=\frac{1}{a}||N[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},u^{0}v^{i}L_{X}]\rho||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{a}||N[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},u^{0}v^{i}L_{X}]\delta\rho||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left(||Nu^{0}v^{i}X^{k}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\nabla[\gamma]_{k}]\rho||_{L^{2}}+(1+||N-1||_{L^{\infty}})||[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},u^{0}v^{i}X^{k}]\nabla[\gamma]_{k}\rho||_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}\left(||v||_{L^{\infty}}(1+||Nu^{0}-1||_{L^{\infty}})||X||_{L^{\infty}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}+(1+||N-1||_{L^{\infty}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.(||\nabla[\gamma](Nu^{0}vX)||_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla\rho||_{H^{s-2}}+||Nu^{0}vX||_{H^{s-1}}||\nabla[\gamma]\rho]||_{L^{\infty}})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{a}(1+||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}}))||X||_{H^{s+1}}||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}||v||_{H^{s-1}}.

The estimates of the terms
i_{1}-i_{10}
yield the required
L^{2}
estimates for
\mathcal{T}_{2remainder}
. Using these estimates, we may also estimate the following term that appears in the energy integral

\displaystyle IM_{2}=|\int_{\Sigma}\frac{2g(v,\dot{v})}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}g(v,\frac{\mathcal{T}_{2}}{u^{0}})\mu_{g}|

(201)

\displaystyle=|\int_{\Sigma}\frac{2\gamma(v,\dot{v})+2u(v,\dot{v})}{[1+g(v,v)]}N\left(\gamma(v,\mathcal{T}_{2})+u(v,\mathcal{T}_{2})\right)\mu_{g}|

\displaystyle\lesssim||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\left(||\dot{v}||_{L^{2}}||N\mathcal{T}_{2}||_{L^{2}}\right)+||u||_{L^{\infty}}||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\left(||\dot{v}||_{L^{2}}||N\mathcal{T}_{2}||_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+||u||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}||v||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\left(||\dot{v}||_{L^{2}}||N\mathcal{T}_{2}||_{L^{2}}\right)

and since
\mathcal{T}^{i}_{2}=-\gamma_{ad}\rho u^{0}[2N-1-n(\gamma_{ad}-1)]\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\dot{v}^{i}+\mathcal{T}^{i}_{2remainder}
,

\displaystyle||N\mathcal{T}_{2}||_{L^{2}}

(202)

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\dot{a}}{a}(||v||_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s-1}})(1+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}})(1+||N-1||_{H^{s+1}})

\displaystyle+||\mathcal{T}_{2remainder}||_{L^{2}}.

The next term reads

\displaystyle IM_{3}=|\int_{\Sigma}\frac{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}{\rho N}\dot{\rho}\mathcal{T}_{1}N\mu_{g}|\leq||[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}||_{L^{\infty}}||\dot{\rho}||_{L^{2}}||\frac{\mathcal{T}_{1}}{\rho}||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=||[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}-1+1||_{L^{\infty}}||\dot{\rho}||_{L^{2}}||\frac{\mathcal{T}_{1}}{\rho}||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq(1+||[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}-1||_{L^{\infty}})||\dot{\rho}||_{L^{2}}||\frac{\mathcal{T}_{1}}{\rho}||_{L^{2}}.

(203)

In order to estimate
IM_{3}
, we recall the expression of
\mathcal{T}_{1}
from equation (
81
)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{T}_{1}}{\rho}:=\frac{1}{\rho}\left(\underbrace{-[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\partial_{t}]\rho}_{ii_{1}}-\underbrace{[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\frac{\gamma_{ad}\rho N\nabla_{i}}{a[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}]v^{i}}_{ii_{2}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{a}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},L_{X}]\rho}_{ii3}\right.

(204)

\displaystyle\left.\underbrace{-[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\frac{\gamma_{ad}\rho g_{ij}v^{j}\partial_{t}}{1+g(v,v)}]v^{i}}_{ii4}-\underbrace{\frac{1}{a}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\frac{NL_{v}}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}]\rho}_{ii5}+\underbrace{\gamma_{ad}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\frac{\rho v_{i}L_{X}}{a[1+g(v,v)]}]v^{i}}_{ii6}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\underbrace{\mathcal{D}^{s-1}\left[\frac{\gamma_{ad}\rho N}{1+g(v,v)}\left(\frac{1}{a}k^{tr}(v,v)-\frac{\dot{a}}{a}g(v,v)\right)\right]}_{ii7}-\underbrace{\mathcal{D}^{s-1}\left[\frac{\gamma_{ad}N\rho L_{v}N}{a[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}\right]}_{ii8}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\underbrace{-\mathcal{D}^{s-1}\left[\frac{(N-1)n\gamma_{ad}\dot{a}}{a}\rho\right]}_{ii9}\right).

We proceed to estimate
\mathcal{T}_{1}/\rho
exactly the same way, that is, term by term

\displaystyle||ii_{1}||_{L^{2}}=||\frac{-[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\partial_{t}]\rho}{\rho}||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq||\frac{1}{\rho}||_{L^{\infty}}||\sum_{l=1}^{s-1}\mathcal{D}^{s-1-l}((T_{\gamma}\mathcal{D}\cdot\partial_{t}\gamma)\mathcal{D}^{l-1}\rho)||_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C||\frac{1}{\rho}||_{L^{\infty}}||\partial_{t}\gamma||_{H^{s-1}}||\rho||_{H^{s-1}},

\displaystyle ii_{2}=-\frac{1}{\rho}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\frac{\gamma_{ad}\rho N\nabla_{i}}{a[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}]v^{i}

(205)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\rho}[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\frac{\gamma_{ad}\rho N}{a[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}](\nabla[\gamma]_{i}v^{i}+\Gamma[g]^{i}_{ik}-\Gamma[\gamma]^{i}_{ik})v^{k}

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma_{ad}N}{a[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}\left([\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\nabla[\gamma]_{i}]v^{i}+[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},(\Gamma[g]^{\cdot}_{i\cdot}-\Gamma[\gamma]^{\cdot}_{i\cdot})]v^{i}\right)

We write
\frac{\rho N}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}
as follows

\displaystyle\frac{\rho N}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}=(\rho-C_{\rho}+C_{\rho})(N-1+1)\left(\frac{1}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}-1+1\right)

\displaystyle=\left((\rho-C_{\rho})(N-1)+(\rho-C_{\rho})+C_{\rho}(N-1)+C_{\rho}\right)\left(\frac{1}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}-1\right)

\displaystyle+\left((\rho-C_{\rho})(N-1)+(\rho-C_{\rho})+C_{\rho}(N-1)+C_{\rho}\right)

Therefore the commutator reads

\displaystyle[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\frac{\rho N}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}]

\displaystyle=[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\left((\rho-C_{\rho})(N-1)+(\rho-C_{\rho})+C_{\rho}(N-1)+C_{\rho}\right)\left(\frac{1}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}-1\right)

\displaystyle+\left((\rho-C_{\rho})(N-1)+(\rho-C_{\rho})+C_{\rho}(N-1)+C_{\rho}\right)]

\displaystyle=[\mathcal{D}^{s-1},\left(\delta\rho\omega+\delta\rho+C_{\rho}\omega+C_{\rho}\right)\left(\frac{1}{[1+g(v,v)]^{1/2}}-1\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\delta\rho\omega+\delta\rho+C_{\rho}\omega\right)]

which after substituting to the expression (
205
) of
ii_{2}

-
118
), and Sobolev embedding, we may write the following

\displaystyle||\frac{1}{\rho}||_{L^{\infty}}=||\frac{1}{\rho}-\frac{1}{C_{\rho}}+\frac{1}{C_{\rho}}||_{L^{\infty}}\leq C||\rho-C_{\rho}||_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{1}{C_{\rho}}\leq C(1+||\delta\rho||_{H^{s-1}}),

\displaystyle||\frac{1}{N}||_{L^{\infty}}=||\frac{1}{N}-1+1||_{L^{\infty}}\leq 1+C||N-1||_{H^{s+1}}=1+C||\omega||_{H^{s+1}},

(216)

\displaystyle||\sqrt{1+g(v,v)}||_{L^{\infty}}\leq 1+C||v||^{2}_{H^{s-1}}(1+||u||_{H^{s}})

which take care of the terms

. In addition, we also note the following estimate using the evolution equation of the metric

(
